\Big\|\sup_{j\in\mathbb{Z}}\big\|\varrho_{k,w_{\ast}}(v+w_{\ast})A_{k}\big[H^{\ast}_{1k}(v,\cdot)\big](w)\big\|_{L^{2}(v\in[j,j+2])}\Big\|_{L^{2}(w\in\mathbb{R})}\lesssim|k|^{-1}.

(7.56)

Similarly we have

\Big\|\sup_{j\in\mathbb{Z}}|k|^{-1}\big\|\varrho_{k,w_{\ast}}(v+w_{\ast})\partial_{v}A_{k}\big[H^{\ast}_{1k}(v,\cdot)\big](w)\big\|_{L^{2}(v\in[j,j+2])}\Big\|_{L^{2}(w\in\mathbb{R})}\lesssim|k|^{-1}.

(7.57)

(
7.56
)-(
7.57
) imply the desired bounds (
7.54
) for
h=A_{k}\big[H^{\ast}_{1k}(v,\cdot)\big](w)
.

The bounds on
A_{k}H^{\ast}_{2k}
is simpler, and we have

\Big\|A\big[H_{2k}^{\ast}(v,\cdot)\big](w)\Big\|_{Y_{k,w_{\ast}}^{+w_{\ast}}}\lesssim|k|^{-1}.

(7.58)

(
7.58
) imply (
7.54
) for
h=A_{k}\big[H^{\ast}_{2k}(v,\cdot)\big](w)
.

We now prove (
7.54
) for the main commutator term
h=\mathcal{C}_{k}^{\ast}(v,w)
. We can decompose

\begin{split}&\mathcal{C}_{k}^{\ast}(v,w)=\mathcal{C}_{k1}^{\ast}(v,w)+\mathcal{C}_{k2}^{\ast}(v,w):=\int_{\mathbb{R}}\mathcal{F}_{k}^{w_{\ast}}(v,\rho,w)\Phi_{k}(\rho)L^{\ast}(\rho,w)A\big[\Theta_{k,\epsilon}^{\iota,w_{\ast}}(\rho,\cdot)\big](w)\,d\rho\\
&-A_{k}\bigg\{\int_{\mathbb{R}}\mathcal{F}_{k}^{w_{\ast}}(v,\rho,\cdot)\Phi_{k}(\rho)L^{\ast}(\rho,w)\Theta_{k,\epsilon}^{\iota,w_{\ast}}(\rho,\cdot)\,d\rho\bigg\}(w)+\mathcal{C}_{k2}^{\ast}(v,w).\end{split}

(7.59)

We first bound
\mathcal{C}_{k2}^{\ast}(v,w)
. Note that in this case the singularity in
\rho
is removed thanks to the function
1-\Phi_{k}(\rho)
.
Define for
\rho\in\mathbb{R}
,

M^{\ast}(\rho):=\Big\|\langle k,\xi\rangle^{-1/2}e^{\delta_{1}\langle k,\xi\rangle^{1/2}}\Big[\big|\widehat{\,\Theta_{k,\epsilon}^{\iota,w_{\ast}}}(\rho,\xi)\big|+|k|^{-1}\big|\partial_{\rho}\widehat{\,\Theta_{k,\epsilon}^{\iota,w_{\ast}}}(\rho,\xi)\big|\Big]\Big\|_{L^{2}(\xi\in\mathbb{R})}.

(7.60)

It follows that for any
\gamma\in(0,1)
and suitable
C_{\gamma}\in(0,\infty)
,

\sup_{j\in\mathbb{Z}}\big\|\varrho_{\varkappa_{k},w_{\ast}}(\rho+w_{\ast})M^{\ast}(\rho)\big\|_{L^{2}(\rho\in[j,j+2])}\lesssim C_{\gamma}+\gamma M.

(7.61)

Define for
\rho,w,\alpha,\beta\in\mathbb{R}

\begin{split}P_{\alpha,\beta}(\rho,w)&:=A_{k}\big[\Theta_{k,\epsilon}^{\iota,w_{\ast}}(\rho,\cdot)\big](w)e^{i(\alpha+\beta)w}-A_{k}\big[\Theta_{k,\epsilon}^{\iota,w_{\ast}}(\rho,\cdot)e^{i(\alpha+\beta)\cdot}\big](w),\\
P^{\ast}_{\alpha,\beta}(\rho,w)&:=\Psi^{\ast}(\rho)P_{\alpha,\beta}(\rho,w).\end{split}

(7.62)

We have the bound

\begin{split}&\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}e^{-(\delta_{0}/2)\langle\alpha\rangle^{1/2}-(\delta_{0}/2)\langle\beta\rangle^{1/2}}\big\|P_{\alpha,\beta}(\rho,w)\big\|_{L^{2}(w\in\mathbb{R})}d\alpha d\beta\\
&\lesssim\Big[\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}e^{-(\delta_{0}/2)(\langle\alpha\rangle^{1/2}+\langle\beta\rangle^{1/2})}\big|e^{\delta_{1}\langle k,\xi-\alpha-\beta\rangle^{1/2}}-e^{\delta_{1}\langle k,\xi\rangle^{1/2}}\big|^{2}\big|\widehat{\,\,\Theta_{k,\epsilon}^{\iota,w_{\ast}}}(\rho,\xi-\alpha-\beta)\big|^{2}\,d\xi d\alpha d\beta\Big]^{1/2}\\
&\lesssim M^{\ast}(\rho),\end{split}

(7.63)

and the bound,

\begin{split}&\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}e^{-(\delta_{0}/2)\langle\alpha\rangle^{1/2}-(\delta_{0}/2)\langle\beta\rangle^{1/2}}\Big\|\widehat{\,\,P^{\ast}_{\alpha,\beta}}(\gamma,w)\Big\|_{L^{2}(w\in\mathbb{R})}\,d\alpha d\beta d\gamma\\
&\lesssim|k|^{1/2}\bigg[\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}e^{-(\delta_{0}/2)(\langle\alpha\rangle^{1/2}+\langle\beta\rangle^{1/2})}\langle\gamma/k\rangle^{2}\big|\widehat{\,\,P^{\ast}_{\alpha,\beta}}(\gamma,w)\big|^{2}\,d\alpha d\beta d\gamma dw\bigg]^{1/2}\\
&\lesssim|k|^{1/2}(C_{\gamma}+\gamma M)\varpi_{\varkappa_{k},w_{\ast}}(w_{\ast},0).\end{split}

(7.64)

Using (
7.49
) and (
7.51
), we can obtain from (
7.61
) that,

\begin{split}&\Big\|\sup_{j\in\mathbb{Z}}\Big[\varrho_{\varkappa_{k},w_{\ast}}(j+w_{\ast})\big\|\Psi^{\ast}(v-j)\mathcal{C}_{k2}^{\ast}(v,w)\big\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R})}\Big]\Big\|_{L^{2}(w\in\mathbb{R})}\\
&\lesssim|k|^{-1}\Big\|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\varrho_{\varkappa_{k},w_{\ast}}(\rho+w_{\ast})(1-\Psi(\rho))e^{-\delta_{0}\langle\alpha\rangle^{1/2}-\delta_{0}\langle\beta\rangle^{1/2}}e^{-2|\rho|}\big|P_{\alpha,\beta}(\rho,w)\big|\,d\alpha d\beta d\rho\Big\|_{L^{2}(w\in\mathbb{R})}\\
&\lesssim|k|^{-1}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\varrho_{\varkappa_{k},w_{\ast}}(\rho+w_{\ast})(1-\Psi(\rho))e^{-2|\rho|}M^{\ast}(\rho)\,d\rho\lesssim\Big[C_{\gamma}+\gamma M\Big]/|k|.\end{split}

(7.65)

Similarly,

|k|^{-1}\Big\|\sup_{j\in\mathbb{Z}}\Big[\varrho_{\varkappa_{k},w_{\ast}}(j+w_{\ast})\big\|\Psi^{\ast}(v-j)\partial_{v}\mathcal{C}_{k2}^{\ast}(v,w)\big\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R})}\Big]\Big\|_{L^{2}(w\in\mathbb{R})}\lesssim\Big[C_{\gamma}+\gamma M\Big]/|k|.

(7.66)

(
7.65
)-(
7.66
) imply that

\big\|\mathcal{C}_{k2}^{\ast}(v,w)\big\|_{Y_{\varkappa_{k},w_{\ast}}^{+w_{\ast}}}\lesssim(C_{\gamma}+\gamma M)/|k|.

(7.67)

In view of the the bounds (
7.49
), the definition (
7.62
) and the bound (
7.64
), we obtain that

\begin{split}&\Big\|\sup_{j\in\mathbb{Z}}\Big[\varrho_{\varkappa_{k},w_{\ast}}(j+w_{\ast})\big\|\Psi^{\ast}(v-j)\mathcal{C}_{k1}^{\ast}(v,w)\big\|_{L^{2}(v\in\mathbb{R})}\Big]\Big\|_{L^{2}(w\in\mathbb{R})}\\
&\lesssim\varrho_{\varkappa_{k},w_{\ast}}(w_{\ast})\Big\|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{4}}\frac{e^{-\delta_{0}(\langle\eta+\alpha_{1}\rangle^{-1/2}+\langle\beta_{1}\rangle^{1/2}+\langle\beta_{2}\rangle^{1/2})}}{\langle k,\eta\rangle^{2}}\big|\widehat{\,\,P^{\ast}_{\beta_{1},\beta_{2}}}(\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{2},w)\big|\,d\alpha d\beta\Big\|_{L^{2}(\eta,w\in\mathbb{R})}\\
&\lesssim|k|^{-1}\big[C_{\gamma}+\gamma M\big],\end{split}

(7.68)

where we used the notations that
d\alpha=d\alpha_{1}d\alpha_{2}
and
d\beta=d\beta_{1}d\beta_{2}
.
Similarly,

|k|^{-1}\Big\|\sup_{j\in\mathbb{Z}}\Big[\varrho_{\varkappa_{k},w_{\ast}}(j+w_{\ast})\big\|\Psi^{\ast}(v-j)\partial_{v}\mathcal{C}_{k1}^{\ast}(v,w)\big\|_{L^{2}(v,w\in\mathbb{R})}\Big]\Big\|_{L^{2}(w\in\mathbb{R})}\lesssim|k|^{-1}\big[C_{\gamma}+\gamma M\big].

(7.69)

The proof of (
7.54
) is then complete. The desired bounds then follow from the limiting absorption principle as in the case
w\geq-15
. We omit the routine details.
∎

8.
Bounds on the spectral density function II: refined bounds

Assume that
k\in\mathbb{Z}\backslash\{0\}
and
|k|\geq 2
. By the bounds (
7.4
)-(
7.5
), (
7.15
) and (
7.45
), the limit

\Gamma_{k}(v,w):=(-i)\lim_{\epsilon\to 0+}\big[\Gamma_{k,\epsilon}^{+}(v,w)-\Gamma_{k,\epsilon}^{-}(v,w)\big]=2\lim_{\epsilon\to 0+}\Im\,\Gamma_{k,\epsilon}^{+}(v,w),

(8.1)

exists at least along a sequence of
\epsilon\to 0+
in the space
L_{\rm loc}(\mathbb{R}^{2})
, and it follows from (
7.3
) that the limit
\Gamma_{k}(v,w)
satisfies the equation for
v,w\in\mathbb{R}
,

(k^{2}-\partial_{v}^{2})\Gamma_{k}(v,w)+{\rm P.V.}\frac{e^{2v}D(v)\Gamma_{k}(v,w)}{B(v)-B(w)}=-2\pi\frac{e^{2w}\big(D(w)\digamma_{k}(w)-F_{0k}(w)\big)}{B^{\prime}(w)}\delta(v-w).

(8.2)

In the above for
w\in\mathbb{R}
,

\digamma_{k}(w):=\lim_{\epsilon\to 0+}\Im\,\Gamma^{+}_{k,\epsilon}(w,w).

(8.3)

For
w_{\ast}\in\mathbb{R}
and denoting
\digamma_{k}^{w_{\ast}}(w):=\digamma(w)\Psi^{\ast}(w-w_{\ast})
, in view of (
7.15
) and (
7.45
), we have the bounds

\big\|e^{\delta_{1}\langle k,\xi\rangle^{1/2}}\widehat{\,\,\digamma_{k}^{w_{\ast}}}(\xi)\big\|_{L^{2}(\xi\in\mathbb{R})}\lesssim\Big[e^{-\mu_{\varkappa_{k}}|w_{\ast}|}{\bf 1}_{w_{\ast}\leq 0}+e^{-\varkappa_{k}|w_{\ast}|}{\bf 1}_{w_{\ast}\geq 0}\Big](M_{k}^{\dagger}+|\sigma_{k}|).

(8.4)

We briefly discuss the existence in the limits (
8.1
) and (
8.3
). The regularity property of
\Gamma^{+}_{k,\epsilon}(v,w)
for sufficiently small
\epsilon
ensures that we can at least take a sequential limit in
L^{2}_{\rm loc}(\mathbb{R}^{2})
. The fact that the limit is unique follows from general representation theory of self adjoint operators, as a consequence of the existence and uniqueness properties of spectral measures corresponding to the self adjoint operator
L_{k}
(with a smoothing in our case since we passed from the vorticity to the stream function). Alternatively, one can prove more refined bounds on
\partial_{\epsilon}\Gamma^{+}_{k,\epsilon}(v,w)
from which the existence of the limit of
\Gamma^{+}_{k,\epsilon}(v,w)
as
\epsilon\to 0+
follows easily, as in
[
24
]
. In our case, since the existence of a sequential limit is sufficient and our main focus is on the quantitative estimates, we will not go into the details.

From (
8.2
) it is clear, heuristically at least, that
\Gamma_{k}(v,w)
with
v,w\in\mathbb{R}
is generated by the source term
-2\pi\frac{e^{2w}\big(D(w)\digamma_{k}(w)-F_{0k}(w)\big)}{B^{\prime}(w)}\delta(v-w)
and should decay when
v
is away from
w
. (The case
|k|=1
is again special due to the presence of embedded eigenvalue
\lambda=0
for
L_{k}
.) To establish more quantitative bounds in this direction, we use the limiting absorption principle to obtain low regularity bounds, see (
5.24
) and (
5.80
), and the commutator argument for Gevrey regularity estimates.

Denote for
w\in\mathbb{R}
,

\digamma_{k}^{\ast}(w):=-2\pi\frac{e^{2w}\big(D(w)\digamma_{k}(w)-F_{0k}(w)\big)}{B^{\prime}(w)}.

(8.5)

By the assumption (
1.3
) and the bound (
8.4
), we have for
w_{\ast}\in\mathbb{R}
and
\digamma_{k}^{w_{\ast}}(w):=\digamma_{k}^{\ast}(w)\Psi^{\ast}(w-w_{\ast})
,

\big\|e^{\delta_{1}\langle k,\xi\rangle^{1/2}}\widehat{\,\,\digamma_{k}^{w_{\ast}}}(\xi)\big\|_{L^{2}(\xi\in\mathbb{R})}\lesssim\big[e^{-\mu_{\varkappa_{k}}|w_{\ast}|}{\bf 1}_{(-\infty,0]}(w_{\ast})+e^{-(\varkappa_{k}+4)|w_{\ast}|}{\bf 1}_{(0,\infty)}(w_{\ast})\big](M_{k}^{\dagger}+|\sigma_{k}|).

(8.6)

We can reformulate (
8.2
) for small
\epsilon>0
and
w\geq-15
as

\begin{split}&\Gamma_{k}(v,w)+\frac{1}{2|k|}\int_{\mathbb{R}}e^{-|k||v-\rho|}\frac{e^{2\rho}D(\rho)\Gamma_{k}(\rho,w)}{B(\rho)-B(w)+i\epsilon}\,d\rho\\
&=\digamma_{k}^{\ast}(w)\frac{e^{-|k||v-w|}}{2|k|}+\frac{1}{2|k|}\int_{\mathbb{R}}e^{-|k||v-\rho|}\Big[\frac{e^{2\rho}D(\rho)\Gamma_{k}(\rho,w)}{B(\rho)-B(w)+i\epsilon}-{\rm P.V.}\frac{e^{2\rho}D(\rho)\Gamma_{k}(\rho,w)}{B(\rho)-B(w)}\Big]\,d\rho,\end{split}

(8.7)

and for
w\leq-15
as

\begin{split}&\Gamma_{k}(v,w)+\int_{\mathbb{R}}\mathcal{G}_{k}^{w}(v,\rho)\Big[\frac{e^{2\rho}D(\rho)}{B(\rho)-B(w)+i\epsilon}-V_{w}(\rho)\Big]\Gamma_{k}(\rho,w)\,d\rho\\
&=\digamma_{k}^{\ast}(w)\mathcal{G}_{k}^{w}(v,w)+\int_{\mathbb{R}}\mathcal{G}_{k}^{w}\Big[\frac{e^{2\rho}D(\rho)\Gamma_{k}(\rho,w)}{B(\rho)-B(w)+i\epsilon}-{\rm P.V.}\frac{e^{2\rho}D(\rho)\Gamma_{k}(\rho,w)}{B(\rho)-B(w)}\Big]\,d\rho.\end{split}

(8.8)

Proposition 8.1
.

Assume that
k\in\mathbb{Z}\backslash\{0\}
,
|k|\geq 2
, and
\Gamma_{k}(v,w)
are defined as in (
8.1
). For
w_{\ast}\in\mathbb{R}
, define for
v,w\in\mathbb{R}
(for the simplicity of notations)

Q_{k}^{w_{\ast}}(v,w):=\Gamma_{k}(v+w,w)\Psi(w-w_{\ast}).

(8.9)

The we have the following bounds

(i) The case of
w_{\ast}\leq-15
.

\begin{split}&\sup_{j\in\mathbb{Z}}\,\zeta_{\varkappa_{k},w_{\ast}}(j+w_{\ast},w_{\ast})\Big\|\big|A_{k}\big[Q_{k}^{w_{\ast}}(v,\cdot)\big](w)\big|+|k|^{-1}\big|\partial_{v}A_{k}\big[Q_{k}^{w_{\ast}}(v,\cdot)\big](w)\big|\Big\|_{L^{2}(v\in[j,j+2],w\in\mathbb{R})}\\
&\lesssim e^{-\mu_{\varkappa_{k}}|w_{\ast}|}(M_{k}^{\dagger}+|\sigma_{k}|)/|k|;\end{split}

(8.10)

(i) The case of
w_{\ast}\geq-15
.

\begin{split}&\sup_{j\in\mathbb{Z}}\,e^{\varkappa_{k}|j|}\Big\|\big|A_{k}\big[Q_{k}^{w_{\ast}}(v,\cdot)\big](w)\big|+|k|^{-1}\big|\partial_{v}A_{k}\big[Q_{k}^{w_{\ast}}(v,\cdot)\big](w)\big|\Big\|_{L^{2}(v\in[j,j+2],w\in\mathbb{R})}\\
&\lesssim e^{-\varkappa_{k}|w_{\ast}|}(M_{k}^{\dagger}+|\sigma_{k}|)/|k|.\end{split}

(8.11)

Proof.

The proof is similar to the proof of propositions
7.2
and
7.3
using the limiting absorption principle, see (
5.24
) for
w\geq-15
and (
5.80
) for
w\leq-15
, together with the commutator argument, and we will be somewhat brief. Assume that
k\geq 2
and
M_{k}^{\dagger}+|\sigma_{k}|=1
, without loss of generality.

We consider the case
w_{\ast}\geq-15
first. Denote

M:=\sup_{j\in\mathbb{Z}}\,e^{\varkappa_{k}|j-w_{\ast}|}\Big\|\big|A_{k}\big[Q_{k}^{w_{\ast}}(v,\cdot)\big](w)\big|+|k|^{-1}\big|\partial_{v}A_{k}\big[Q_{k}^{w_{\ast}}(v,\cdot)\big](w)\big|\Big\|_{L^{2}(v\in[j,j+2],w\in\mathbb{R})}.

We need to show that
M\lesssim 1/|k|
. From (
8.7
), it follows that
Q_{k}^{w_{\ast}}
satisfies for
v,w\in\mathbb{R}
and
0<\epsilon\ll e^{-2|w_{\ast}|}
,

\begin{split}&Q_{k}^{w_{\ast}}(v,w)+\frac{1}{2|k|}\int_{\mathbb{R}}e^{-|k||v-\rho|}\frac{e^{2\rho+2w}D(\rho+w)}{B(\rho+w)-B(w)+i\epsilon}Q_{k}^{w_{\ast}}(\rho,w)\,d\rho\\
&=\digamma_{k}^{\ast}(w)\frac{e^{-|k||v|}}{2|k|}+J_{1\epsilon}(v,w)+J_{2\epsilon}(v,w),\end{split}

(8.12)

where we have denoted

\begin{split}&J_{1,\epsilon}(v,w):=\frac{1}{2|k|}\int_{\mathbb{R}}e^{-|k||v-\rho|}\big(1-\Psi(\rho)\big)\Psi^{\ast}(w-w_{\ast})\Big[\frac{e^{2\rho+2w}D(\rho+w)Q_{k}^{w_{\ast}}(\rho,w)}{B(\rho+w)-B(w)+i\epsilon}\\
&\hskip 216.81pt-{\rm P.V.}\frac{e^{2\rho+2w}D(\rho+w)Q_{k}^{w_{\ast}}(\rho,w)}{B(\rho+w)-B(w)}\Big]\,d\rho,\\
&J_{2\epsilon}(v,w):=\frac{1}{2|k|}\int_{\mathbb{R}}e^{-|k||v-\rho|}\Psi(\rho)\Psi^{\ast}(w-w_{\ast})\Big[\frac{e^{2\rho+2w}D(\rho+w)Q_{k}^{w_{\ast}}(\rho,w)}{B(\rho+w)-B(w)+i\epsilon}\\
&\hskip 216.81pt-{\rm P.V.}\frac{e^{2\rho}D(\rho)Q_{k}^{w_{\ast}}(\rho,w)}{B(\rho+w)-B(w)}\Big]\,d\rho.\end{split}

(8.13)

In the above we suppressed the dependence of
J_{1\epsilon},J_{2\epsilon}
on
k,w_{\ast}
for the simplicity of notations. It follows from the bounds (
7.5
) that for
w\in\mathbb{R}
,

\Big\|\sup_{j\in\mathbb{Z}}\Big\|e^{\varkappa_{k}|v|}\Big[\big|J_{1\epsilon}(v,w)\big|+|k|^{-1}\big|\partial_{v}J_{1\epsilon}(v,w)\big|\Big]\Big\|_{L^{2}(v\in[j,j+2])}\Big\|_{L^{2}(w\in\mathbb{R})}\lesssim_{k,w_{\ast}}\epsilon,

(8.14)

where we do not have to be precise on the implied constants which might depend on
k,w_{\ast}
since in the end
\epsilon\to 0+
.
For
w_{\ast}\in\mathbb{R}
, we notice that the bounds (
7.15
) imply that

\Big\|\Psi^{\ast}(v)\Big[\big|A_{k}\big[Q_{k}^{w_{\ast}}(v,\cdot)\big](w)\big|+|k|^{-1}\big|\partial_{v}A_{k}\big[Q_{k}^{w_{\ast}}(v,\cdot)\big](w)\big|\Big]\Big\|_{L^{2}(v\in\mathbb{R},w\in\mathbb{R})}\lesssim e^{-\varkappa_{k}|w_{\ast}|}/|k|.

(8.15)

Using lemma
2.3
, (
7.36
) and (
8.15
), we have

\begin{split}&\Big\|\sup_{j\in\mathbb{Z}}\Big\|e^{|k||j|}\Psi^{\ast}(v-j)\Big[\big|J_{2}(v,w)\big|+|k|^{-1}\big|\partial_{v}J_{2}(v,w)\big|\Big]\Big\|_{L^{2}(v\in[j,j+2])}\Big\|_{L^{2}(w\in\mathbb{R})}\\
&\lesssim|k|^{-1}\Big\|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\langle k,\eta\rangle^{-1}e^{-\langle\eta+\alpha\rangle^{3/4}-\delta_{0}\langle\beta\rangle^{1/2}}\Big|\int_{\mathbb{R}}e^{i(\alpha+\gamma)\rho}Q_{k}^{w_{\ast}}(\rho,w)\,d\rho\Big|d\alpha d\beta d\gamma\Big\|_{L^{2}(\eta\in\mathbb{R},w\in\mathbb{R})}\\
&\lesssim e^{-|\varkappa_{k}||w_{\ast}|}/|k|.\end{split}

(8.16)

By the limiting absorption principle for each
w\in\mathbb{R}
with
|w-w_{\ast}|\leq 5
, see (
5.24
) (with a shift in
v\to v+w
), and square integrating in
w
, we conclude from (
8.12
) and the bounds (
8.14
)-(
8.16
),

\Big\|\sup_{j\in\mathbb{Z}}\Big\|e^{\varkappa_{k}|v|}\Big[\big|Q_{k}^{w_{\ast}}(v,w)\big|+|k|^{-1}\big|\partial_{v}Q_{k}^{w_{\ast}}(v,w)\big|\Big]\Big\|_{L^{2}(v\in[j,j+2])}\Big\|_{L^{2}(w\in\mathbb{R})}\lesssim C_{k,w_{\ast}}\epsilon+|k|^{-1}e^{-\varkappa_{k}|w_{\ast}|}.

(8.17)

Sending
\epsilon\to 0+
, we have,

\Big\|\sup_{j\in\mathbb{Z}}\Big\|e^{\varkappa_{k}|v|}\Big[\big|Q_{k}^{w_{\ast}}(v,w)\big|+|k|^{-1}\big|\partial_{v}Q_{k}^{w_{\ast}}(v,w)\big|\Big]\Big\|_{L^{2}(v\in[j,j+2])}\Big\|_{L^{2}(w\in\mathbb{R})}\lesssim|k|^{-1}e^{-\varkappa_{k}|w_{\ast}|}.

(8.18)

To prove the higher regularity estimates, we again use the commutator argument, apply the Fourier multiplier
A_{k}
to equation (
8.12
), multiply with
\Psi^{\ast}(w-w_{\ast})
, and obtain that

\begin{split}&\Psi^{\ast}(w-w_{\ast})A_{k}\big[Q_{k}^{w_{\ast}}(v,\cdot)\big](w)+\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{e^{-|k||v-\rho|}}{2|k|}\frac{e^{2\rho+2w}D(\rho+w)\Psi^{\ast}(w-w_{\ast})}{B(\rho+w)-B(w)+i\epsilon}A_{k}\big[Q_{k}^{w_{\ast}}(\rho,\cdot)\big](w)\,d\rho\\
&=\Psi^{\ast}(w-w_{\ast})\Big\{A_{k}\big[\digamma^{\ast}_{k}(\cdot)\big](w)\frac{e^{-|k||v|}}{2|k|}+A_{k}\big[J_{1\epsilon}(v,\cdot)\big](w)+A_{k}\big[J_{2\epsilon}(v,\cdot)\big](w)+\mathcal{C}_{Q}(v,w)\Big\},\end{split}

(8.19)

where the commutator term
\mathcal{C}_{Q}
(we have again suppressed the dependence on
k,w_{\ast}
for the simplicity of notations) is given by (recall the definitions (
7.20
) and the bound (
7.21
) for the kernel
K
)

\begin{split}\mathcal{C}_{Q}(v,w):=&\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{e^{-|k||v-\rho|}}{2|k|}e^{2\rho+2w_{\ast}}\frac{1}{B^{\prime}(w_{\ast})}\Big\{K(\rho,w)A_{k}\big[Q_{k}^{w_{\ast}}(\rho,\cdot)\big](w)-A_{k}\big[K(\rho,\cdot)Q_{k}^{w_{\ast}}(\rho,\cdot)\big](w)\Big\}\,d\rho.\end{split}

(8.20)

Straightforward calculations using (
7.15
) show that

\begin{split}&\sup_{j\in\mathbb{Z}}\Big\|e^{\varkappa_{k}|v|}\Big\{\big|A_{k}\big[\digamma^{\ast}_{k}(\cdot)\big](w)\big|+|k|^{-1}\big|\partial_{v}A_{k}\big[\digamma^{\ast}_{k}(\cdot)\big](w)\big|\Big\}\Big\|_{L^{2}(v\in[j,j+2]),w\in\mathbb{R}}\lesssim e^{-\varkappa_{k}|w_{\ast}|}/|k|,\\
&\sup_{j\in\mathbb{Z}}\Big\|e^{\varkappa_{k}|v|}\Big[\big|A_{k}\big[J_{1}(v,\cdot)\big](w)\big|+|k|^{-1}\big|\partial_{v}A_{k}\big[J_{1}(v,\cdot)\big](w)\big|\Big]\Big\|_{L^{2}(v\in[j,j+2],w\in\mathbb{R})}\lesssim_{k,w_{\ast}}\epsilon.\end{split}

(8.21)

Similar calculations as in (
7.27
), see the bounds (
7.26
) on
h_{0k}^{1\ast}
, we have

\begin{split}&\Big\|\sup_{j\in\mathbb{Z}}\Big\|e^{\varkappa_{k}|v|}\Big\{\big|A_{k}\big[J_{2}(v,\cdot)\big](w)\big|+|k|^{-1}\big|\partial_{v}A_{k}\big[J_{2}(v,\cdot)\big](w)\big|\Big\}\Big\|_{L^{2}(v\in[j,j+2])}\Big\|_{L^{2}(w\in\mathbb{R})}\lesssim e^{-\varkappa_{k}|w_{\ast}|}/|k|,\end{split}

(8.22)

Additionally, using the same argument as in the bounds for
\mathcal{C}_{k}(v,w)
, see (
7.38
)-(
7.39
), we can bound for any
\gamma\in(0,1)
and suitable
C_{\gamma}\in(0,\infty)
,

\begin{split}&\Big\|\sup_{j\in\mathbb{Z}}\Big\|e^{\varkappa_{k}|v|}\Big[\big|\mathcal{C}_{Q}(v,w)\big|+|k|^{-1}\big|\partial_{v}\mathcal{C}_{Q}(v,w)\big|\Big]\Big\|_{L^{2}(v\in[j,j+2])}\Big\|_{L^{2}(w\in\mathbb{R})}\lesssim(C_{\gamma}e^{-\varkappa_{k}|w_{\ast}|}+\gamma M)/|k|.\end{split}

(8.23)

Combining the bounds (
8.21
)-(
8.23
), applying the limiting absorption principle (see (
5.24
)) for each
w
with
|w-w_{\ast}|\leq 5
, square integrating in
w
, and comparing
\Psi^{\ast}(w-w_{\ast})A_{k}\big[Q_{k}^{w_{\ast}}(v,\cdot)\big](w)
and
A_{k}\big[Q_{k}^{w_{\ast}}(v,\cdot)\big](w)
, we can conclude that

M\lesssim(C_{\gamma}e^{-|k||w_{\ast}|}+\gamma M)/|k|.

(8.24)

Choosing
\gamma\in(0,1)
sufficiently small, then the desired bounds (
8.11
) follow from (
8.24
).

We now turn to the proof of (
8.10
) for the case
w_{\ast}\in(-\infty,-15]
. Denote

M^{\ast}:=\sup_{j\in\mathbb{Z}}\,\zeta_{\varkappa_{k},w_{\ast}}(j+w_{\ast},w_{\ast})\Big\|\big|A_{k}\big[Q_{k}^{w_{\ast}}(v,\cdot)\big](w)\big|+|k|^{-1}\big|\partial_{v}A_{k}\big[Q_{k}^{w_{\ast}}(v,\cdot)\big](w)\big|\Big\|_{L^{2}(v\in[j,j+2],w\in\mathbb{R})}.

We need to show that
M^{\ast}\lesssim 1/|k|
. Denote for
v,w\in\mathbb{R}
,

\digamma_{k}^{\dagger}(v,w):=-2\frac{e^{2w}\big(D(w)\digamma_{k}(w)-F_{0k}(w)\big)}{B^{\prime}(w)}\mathcal{G}_{k}^{w}(v+w,w).

(8.25)

By the assumption (
1.3
), the bounds (
8.4
) and lemma
2.3
, we have the bounds for
w_{\ast}\in(-\infty,-15]
and
\digamma_{k,w_{\ast}}^{\dagger}(v,w):=\digamma_{k}^{\dagger}(v,w)\Psi^{\ast}(w-w_{\ast})
,

\Big\|e^{\delta_{1}\langle k,\xi\rangle^{1/2}}\widehat{\,\,\digamma_{k,w_{\ast}}^{\dagger}}(v,\xi)\Big\|_{L^{2}(\xi\in\mathbb{R})}\lesssim\varpi_{k,w_{\ast}}(v+w_{\ast},w_{\ast})e^{-\mu_{\varkappa_{k}}|w_{\ast}|}.

(8.26)

It follows from equation (
8.8
) that
Q_{k}^{w_{\ast}}
satisfies the equation

\begin{split}&Q_{k}^{w_{\ast}}(v,w)+\int_{\mathbb{R}}\mathcal{G}_{k}^{w}(v+w,\rho+w)\Big[\frac{e^{2\rho+2w}D(\rho+w)}{B(\rho+w)-B(w)+i\epsilon}-V_{w}(\rho+w)\Big]Q_{k}^{w_{\ast}}(\rho,w)\,d\rho\\
&=\digamma_{k,w_{\ast}}^{\dagger}(v,w)+J^{\ast}_{1\epsilon}(v,w)+J^{\ast}_{2\epsilon}(v,w),\end{split}

(8.27)

where we have denoted

\begin{split}&J^{\ast}_{1\epsilon}(v,w):=\int_{\mathbb{R}}\mathcal{G}_{k}^{w}(v+w,\rho+w)\big(1-\Psi(\rho)\big)\Psi^{\ast}(w-w_{\ast})\Big[\frac{e^{2\rho+2w}D(\rho+w)Q_{k}^{w_{\ast}}(\rho,w)}{B(\rho+w)-B(w)+i\epsilon}\\
&\hskip 216.81pt-{\rm P.V.}\frac{e^{2\rho+2w}D(\rho+w)Q_{k}^{w_{\ast}}(\rho,w)}{B(\rho+w)-B(w)}\Big]\,d\rho,\\
&J^{\ast}_{2\epsilon}(v,w):=\int_{\mathbb{R}}\mathcal{G}_{k}^{w}(v+w,\rho+w)\Psi(\rho)\Psi^{\ast}(w-w_{\ast})\Big[\frac{e^{2\rho+2w}D(\rho+w)Q_{k}^{w_{\ast}}(\rho,w)}{B(\rho+w)-B(w)+i\epsilon}\\
&\hskip 216.81pt-{\rm P.V.}\frac{e^{2\rho+2w}D(\rho+w)Q_{k}^{w_{\ast}}\rho,w)}{B(\rho+w)-B(w)}\Big]\,d\rho.\end{split}

(8.28)

In the above we suppressed the dependence of
J^{\ast}_{1\epsilon},J^{\ast}_{2\epsilon}
on
k,w_{\ast}
. Simple calculations (as we do not need precise dependence on
k,w_{\ast}
) using the bounds (
7.4
) show that

\Big\|\sup_{j\in\mathbb{Z}}\Big\|\zeta_{\varkappa_{k},w_{\ast}}(v+w_{\ast},w_{\ast})\Big[\big|J^{\ast}_{1\epsilon}(v,w)\big|+|k|^{-1}\big|\partial_{v}J^{\ast}_{1\epsilon}(v,w)\big|\Big]\Big\|_{L^{2}(v\in[j,j+2])}\Big\|_{L^{2}(w\in\mathbb{R})}\lesssim_{k,w_{\ast}}\epsilon.

(8.29)

Calculations similar to (
7.55
)-(
7.56
), using (
3.4
), show that

\Big\|\sup_{j\in\mathbb{Z}}\Big\|\zeta_{\varkappa_{k},w_{\ast}}(v+w_{\ast},w_{\ast})\Big[\big|J^{\ast}_{2\epsilon}(v,w)\big|+|k|^{-1}\big|\partial_{v}J^{\ast}_{2\epsilon}(v,w)\big|\Big]\Big\|_{L^{2}(v\in[j,j+2])}\Big\|_{L^{2}(w\in\mathbb{R})}\lesssim\frac{e^{-\mu_{\varkappa_{k}}|w_{\ast}|}}{|k|}.

(8.30)

By the limiting absorption principle for
w\in\mathbb{R}
with
|w-w_{\ast}|\leq 5
, see (
5.80
) (with a shift in
v\to v+w_{\ast}
), and square integrating in
w
, we conclude from (
8.12
) and the bounds (
8.14
)-(
8.16
) that

\Big\|\sup_{j\in\mathbb{Z}}\Big\|\zeta_{\varkappa_{k},w_{\ast}}(v+w_{\ast},w_{\ast})\Big[\big|Q_{k}^{w_{\ast}}(v,w)\big|+\frac{1}{|k|}\big|\partial_{v}Q_{k}^{w_{\ast}}(v,w)\big|\Big]\Big\|_{L^{2}(v\in[j,j+2])}\Big\|_{L^{2}(w\in\mathbb{R})}\lesssim C_{w_{\ast}}\epsilon+\frac{e^{-\mu_{\varkappa_{k}}|w_{\ast}|}}{|k|}.

(8.31)

Sending
\epsilon\to 0+
, we obtain that

\Big\|\sup_{j\in\mathbb{Z}}\Big\|\zeta_{\varkappa_{k},w_{\ast}}(v+w_{\ast},w_{\ast})\Big[\big|Q_{k}^{w_{\ast}}(v,w)\big|+|k|^{-1}\big|\partial_{v}Q_{k}^{w_{\ast}}(v,w)\big|\Big]\Big\|_{L^{2}(v\in[j,j+2])}\Big\|_{L^{2}(w\in\mathbb{R})}\lesssim e^{-\mu_{\varkappa_{k}}|w_{\ast}|}/|k|.

(8.32)

To prove the higher regularity estimates, we again use the commutator argument, apply the Fourier multiplier
A_{k}
to equation (
8.12
) and multiply with
\Psi^{\ast}(w-w_{\ast})
, and obtain that

\begin{split}&\Psi^{\ast}(w-w_{\ast})A_{k}\big[Q_{k}^{w_{\ast}}(v,\cdot)\big](w)+\int_{\mathbb{R}}\mathcal{F}_{k}(v,\rho,w)\frac{e^{2\rho+2w}D(\rho+w)\Psi^{\ast}(w-w_{\ast})}{B(\rho+w)-B(w)+i\epsilon}A_{k}\big[Q_{k}^{w_{\ast}}(\rho,\cdot)\big](w)\,d\rho\\
&=\Psi^{\ast}(w-w_{\ast})\Big\{A_{k}\big[\digamma^{\dagger}_{k}(v,\cdot)\big](w)+A_{k}\big[J^{\ast}_{1}(v,\cdot)\big](w)+A_{k}\big[J^{\ast}_{2}(v,\cdot)\big](w)+\mathcal{C}^{\ast}_{Q}(v,w)\Big\},\end{split}

(8.33)

where the commutator term
\mathcal{C}^{\ast}_{Q}
(we have again suppressed the dependence on
k,w_{\ast}
for the simplicity of notations) is given by

\begin{split}\mathcal{C}^{\ast}_{Q}(v,w):=&\int_{\mathbb{R}}\mathcal{F}_{k}^{w_{\ast}}(v,\rho,w)L(\rho,w)A_{k}\big[Q_{k}^{w_{\ast}}(\rho,\cdot)\big](w)\,d\rho-A_{k}\Big\{\int_{\mathbb{R}}\mathcal{F}_{k}^{w_{\ast}}(v,\rho,\cdot)L(\rho,\cdot)Q_{k}^{w_{\ast}}(\rho,\cdot)\,d\rho\Big\}.\end{split}

(8.34)

In the above we used the definitions (
3.36
) for
\mathcal{F}_{k}
, (
7.48
) for
\mathcal{F}_{k}^{w_{\ast}}
, and recall (
7.50
)-(
7.51
) for the definitions and bounds of
L
and
L^{\ast}
.
Simple calculations show that

\begin{split}&\Big\|\sup_{j\in\mathbb{Z}}\,\zeta_{\varkappa_{k},w_{\ast}}(j+w_{\ast},w_{\ast})\Big\|\big|A_{k}\big[\digamma^{\dagger}_{k}(v,\cdot)\big](w)\big|+|k|^{-1}\big|\partial_{v}A_{k}\big[\digamma^{\dagger}_{k}(v,\cdot)\big](w)\big|\Big\|_{L^{2}(v\in[j,j+2])}\Big\|_{L^{2}(w\in\mathbb{R})}\\
&\lesssim e^{-\mu_{\varkappa_{k}}|w_{\ast}|}/|k|,\\
&\Big\|\sup_{j\in\mathbb{Z}}\,\zeta_{\varkappa_{k},w_{\ast}}(j+w_{\ast},w_{\ast})\Big\|\big|A_{k}\big[J^{\ast}_{1}(v,\cdot)\big](w)\big|+|k|^{-1}\big|\partial_{v}A_{k}\big[J^{\ast}_{1}(v,\cdot)\big](w)\big|\Big\|_{L^{2}(v\in[j,j+2])}\Big\|_{L^{2}(w\in\mathbb{R})}\\
&\lesssim_{k,w_{\ast}}\epsilon.\end{split}

(8.35)

Calculations similar to (
7.55
) show that

\begin{split}&\Big\|\sup_{j\in\mathbb{Z}}\,\zeta_{\varkappa_{k},w_{\ast}}(j+w_{\ast},w_{\ast})\Big\|\big|A_{k}\big[J^{\ast}_{2}(v,\cdot)\big](w)\big|+|k|^{-1}\big|\partial_{v}A_{k}\big[J^{\ast}_{2}(v,\cdot)\big](w)\big|\Big\|_{L^{2}(v\in[j,j+2])}\Big\|_{L^{2}(w\in\mathbb{R})}\\
&\lesssim e^{-\mu_{\varkappa_{k}}|w_{\ast}|}/|k|.\end{split}

(8.36)

Additionally, we can bound for any
\gamma\in(0,1)
, completely analogous to (
7.59
)-(
7.68
), that

\begin{split}&\Big\|\sup_{j\in\mathbb{Z}}\Big\|\zeta_{\varkappa_{k},w_{\ast}}(j+w_{\ast},w_{\ast})\Big[\big|\mathcal{C}_{Q}^{\ast}(v,w)\big|+|k|^{-1}\big|\partial_{v}\mathcal{C}_{Q}^{\ast}(v,w)\big|\Big]\Big\|_{L^{2}(v\in[j,j+2])}\Big\|_{L^{2}(w\in\mathbb{R})}\\
&\lesssim(C_{\gamma}e^{-\mu_{\varkappa_{k}}|w_{\ast}|}+\gamma M^{\ast})/|k|.\end{split}

(8.37)

Combining (
8.37
) with the bounds (
8.21
)-(
8.23
), applying the limiting absorption principle, and comparing
\Psi^{\ast}(w-w_{\ast})A_{k}\big[Q_{k}^{w_{\ast}}(v,\cdot)\big](w)
and
A_{k}\big[Q_{k}^{w_{\ast}}(v,\cdot)\big](w)
, we can conclude that

M^{\ast}\lesssim(C_{\gamma}e^{-\mu_{\varkappa_{k}}|w_{\ast}|}+\gamma M^{\ast})/|k|.

(8.38)

Choosing
\gamma\in(0,1)
sufficiently small, then the desired bounds (
8.10
) follow from (
8.38
).

∎

9.
Proof of main theorems

In this section we give the proof of our main theorems, beginning with the proof of Theorem
1.4
.

9.1.
Proof of Theorem
1.4

We can normalize and assume that
M_{k}^{\dagger}+|\sigma_{k}|=1
. In view of the equation (
8.2
), the bounds (
8.4
), and the bounds (
8.10
)-(
8.11
), it remains to prove (
1.47
) and (
1.53
). it follows from (
8.2
) that for
v,w\in\mathbb{R}
with
|v|\geq\frac{1}{2}
,
\Theta_{k}(v,w)
satisfies

(k^{2}-\partial_{v}^{2})\Theta_{k}(v,w)+\frac{e^{2v+2w}D(v+w)}{B(v+w)-B(w)}\Theta_{k}(v,w)=0.

(9.1)

We note that for
|v|\geq 1/2
the coefficient
\frac{e^{2v+2w}D(v+w)}{B(v+w)-B(w)}
is Gevrey-2 regular in both
v
and
w
. The desired bounds (
1.47
) then follow from the bounds (
8.10
)-(
8.11
), and lemma
2.5
applied to (
9.1
) for each
w\in\mathbb{R}
with
|w-w_{\ast}|\leq 5
and then square integrated in
w
.

We now turn to the proof of (
1.52
)-(
1.53
). Choose a smooth function
\Psi_{9}\in C^{\infty}_{0}(-10,10)
with
\Psi_{9}\equiv 1
on
[-9,9]
and
\sup_{\xi\in\mathbb{R}}e^{\langle\xi\rangle^{5/6}}\big|\widehat{\Psi_{9}}(\xi)\big|\lesssim 1
. Fix a small parameter
\sigma\in(0,1)
to be determined below.
For notational conveniences, we assume that
\zeta(v)
is extended to be defined on
\mathbb{R}
, satisfying
|\zeta^{\prime}(v)|\approx 1
on
\mathbb{R}
and
\zeta^{\prime}\in\widetilde{G}_{M^{\prime}}^{1/2}(\mathbb{R})
with a suitable
M^{\prime}\in(0,\infty)
depending on
M
.
Define for
v,\rho,w\in\mathbb{R}
,

P_{k}(v,\rho,w):=\Theta_{k}(\zeta(v+\rho)-\zeta(\rho),w),\,\,P^{\ast}_{k}(v,\rho,w):=P(v,\rho,w)\Psi^{\ast}(w-w_{\ast})\Psi^{\ast}(v/\sigma)\Psi^{\ast}(\rho),

(9.2)

then
P_{k}^{\ast}
satisfies for
v,\rho,w\in\mathbb{R}
,

\begin{split}&\Big[k^{2}-(\zeta^{\prime}(v+\rho))^{-2}\partial_{v}^{2}+\frac{\zeta^{\prime\prime}(v+\rho)}{(\zeta^{\prime}(v+\rho))^{3}}\partial_{v}\Big]P_{k}^{\ast}(v,\rho,w)+{\rm P.V.}\,\frac{a^{\ast}(v,\rho,w)}{v}P_{k}^{\ast}(v,\rho,w)=R_{\sigma}(v,\rho,w),\end{split}

(9.3)

where in the above

\begin{split}&a^{\ast}(v,\rho,w):=v\frac{e^{2\zeta(v+\rho)-2\zeta(\rho)+2w}D(\zeta(v+\rho)-\zeta(\rho)+w)}{B\big(\zeta(v+\rho)-\zeta(\rho)+w\big)-B(w)}\Psi_{9}(v)\Psi_{9}(\rho)\Psi_{9}(w-w_{\ast}),\\
&R_{\sigma}(v,\rho,w):=(1/\sigma)\frac{\zeta^{\prime\prime}(v+\rho)}{(\zeta^{\prime}(v+\rho))^{3}}\partial_{v}\Psi^{\ast}(v/\sigma)P_{k}(v,\rho,w)\Psi^{\ast}(\rho)\Psi^{\ast}(w-w_{\ast})\\
&-(\zeta^{\prime}(v+\rho))^{-2}\Big[(2/\sigma)\partial_{v}P(v,\rho,w)\partial_{v}\Psi^{\ast}(v/\sigma)+(1/\sigma^{2})P(v,\rho,w)\partial_{v}^{2}\Psi^{\ast}(v/\sigma)\Big]\Psi^{\ast}(\rho)\Psi^{\ast}(w-w_{\ast})\\
&-\frac{2\pi e^{2w}(D(w)\digamma_{k}(w)-F_{0k}(w))}{B^{\prime}(w)\zeta^{\prime}(\rho)}\Psi^{\ast}(\rho)\Psi^{\ast}(w-w_{\ast})\delta(v).\end{split}

(9.4)

The main point of the equation (
9.3
) is that
R_{\sigma}(v,\rho,w)
is Gevrey regular in both
\rho
and
w
, and the coefficient
a^{\ast}(v,\rho,w)
is Gevrey-2 smooth in both
v,\rho
and
w
for
|v|\leq 20,|\rho|\leq 20
and
|w-w_{\ast}|\leq 20
. Indeed, we have for some
\delta_{0}^{\prime}\in(0,1)
depending on
M
and
\delta_{0}
,

\big\|e^{\delta_{0}^{\prime}\langle\alpha,\beta,\gamma\rangle^{1/2}}\widehat{\,a^{\ast}}(\alpha,\beta,\gamma)\big\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\lesssim 1.

(9.5)

To see (
9.5
), we can rewrite

\begin{split}a^{\ast}(v,\rho,w):=&e^{2\zeta(v+\rho)-2\zeta(\rho)+2w}D(\zeta(v+\rho)-\zeta(\rho)+w)\frac{v}{\zeta(v+\rho)-\zeta(\rho)}\\
&\times\frac{\zeta(v+\rho)-\zeta(\rho)}{B\big(\zeta(v+\rho)-\zeta(\rho)+w\big)-B(w)}\Psi_{9}(\rho)\Psi_{9}(w-w_{\ast})\Psi_{9}(v),\end{split}

(9.6)

and the desired bound (
9.5
) follows from the simple observation that for
v,\rho,\alpha,w\in\mathbb{R}
,

\zeta(v+\rho)-\zeta(\rho)=v\int_{0}^{1}\zeta^{\prime}(\rho+sv)\,ds,\quad{\rm and}\quad B(\alpha+w)-B(w):=\alpha\int_{0}^{1}B^{\prime}(w+s\alpha)\,ds.

(9.7)

Moreover, since
\partial_{v}\Psi^{\ast}(v/\sigma)
and
\partial_{v}^{2}\Psi^{\ast}(v/\sigma)
are supported away from
v=0
, the right hand side
R
of (
9.3
) is Gevrey-2 regular in
v,\rho
and
w
for
|v|\leq 20,|\rho|\leq 20
and
|w-w_{\ast}|\leq 20
.

Now to prove the desired Gevrey smoothness in
\rho
for
P^{\ast}
, we use the same commutator argument as before. It seems at first glance that we are in the same situation as in propositions
7.2
and
7.3
, and would need to apply the limiting absorption principle, see Lemmas
5.2
and
5.6
, which involves weights and is quite complicated. However, in our case here, there is a key difference, namely we already know that
P
is Gevrey smooth whenever
v
stays away from the origin, and that allows us to localize
v
to a small region near
v=0
and the extra small parameter
\sigma
ensures coercive bounds from (
9.3
), without the use of the limiting absorption principle.

By the bounds (
1.49
), we have

\big\|(|k|+|\alpha|)e^{\delta_{1}\langle k,\gamma\rangle^{1/2}}\widehat{\,\,P_{k}^{\ast}}(\alpha,\beta,\gamma)\big\|_{L^{2}(\alpha,\beta,\gamma\in\mathbb{R})}\lesssim_{\sigma}\varpi_{\varkappa_{k},w_{\ast}}(w_{\ast},0),

(9.8)

which we will use to control the low frequency part (for the variable
\rho
) of the desired bound (
1.53
).

By the bounds (
9.8
), equation (
9.1
) and lemma
2.5
, we can choose
\delta_{1}^{\prime}\in(0,1)
sufficiently small, depending on
\delta_{1}
and
M
, such that

\sup_{\alpha\in\mathbb{R}}\big\|e^{2\delta_{1}^{\prime}\langle k,\beta\rangle^{1/2}+2\delta_{1}^{\prime}\langle k,\gamma\rangle^{1/2}}\widehat{\,R_{\sigma}}(\alpha,\beta,\gamma)\big\|_{L^{2}(\beta,\gamma\in\mathbb{R})}\lesssim_{\sigma}(M_{k}^{\dagger}+|\sigma_{k}|)\varpi_{\varkappa_{k},w_{\ast}}(w_{\ast},0).

(9.9)

Define the Fourier multiplier
A_{k}^{\ast}
such that for any
h\in L^{2}(\mathbb{R})

\widehat{A_{k}^{\ast}h}(\beta):=e^{\delta_{1}^{\prime}\langle k,\beta\rangle^{1/2}}\widehat{\,h\,}(\beta),\qquad{\rm for}\,\,\beta\in\mathbb{R}.

(9.10)

Applying the Fourier multiplier
A_{k}^{\ast}
(in the variables
\rho
) to equation (
9.3
), we obtain that for
v,\rho,w\in\mathbb{R}
,

\begin{split}&\Big[k^{2}-(\zeta^{\prime}(v+\rho))^{-2}\partial_{v}^{2}+\frac{\zeta^{\prime\prime}(v+\rho)}{(\zeta^{\prime}(v+\rho))^{3}}\partial_{v}\Big]A_{k}^{\ast}\big[P_{k}^{\ast}(v,\cdot,w)\big](\rho)+{\rm P.V.}\,\frac{a^{\ast}(v,\rho,w)}{v}A_{k}^{\ast}\big[P_{k}^{\ast}(v,\cdot,w)\big](\rho)\\
&=A_{k}^{\ast}\big[R_{\sigma}(v,\cdot,w)\big](\rho)+C^{11}_{\sigma}(v,\rho,w)+C^{12}_{\sigma}(v,\rho,w)+C^{2}_{\sigma}(v,\rho,w),\end{split}

(9.11)

where we have defined

\begin{split}&C^{11}_{\sigma}(v,\rho,w):=\partial_{v}H(v,\rho,w),\\
&H_{\sigma}(v,\rho,w):=\Big[-(\zeta^{\prime}(v+\rho))^{-2}\partial_{v}\Big]A_{k}^{\ast}\big[P_{k}^{\ast}(v,\cdot,w)\big](\rho)-A_{k}^{\ast}\Big[-(\zeta^{\prime}(v+\cdot))^{-2}\partial_{v}P_{k}^{\ast}(v,\cdot,w)\Big](\rho),\\
&C^{12}_{\sigma}(v,\rho,w):=\Big[\partial_{v}(\zeta^{\prime}(v+\rho))^{-2}\partial_{v}+\frac{\zeta^{\prime\prime}(v+\rho)}{(\zeta^{\prime}(v+\rho))^{3}}\partial_{v}\Big]A_{k}^{\ast}\big[P_{k}^{\ast}(v,\cdot,w)\big](\rho)\\
&\hskip 72.26999pt-A_{k}^{\ast}\Big[\Big(\big(\partial_{v}(\zeta^{\prime}(v+\cdot))^{-2}\partial_{v}\big)+\frac{\zeta^{\prime\prime}(v+\cdot)}{(\zeta^{\prime}(v+\cdot))^{3}}\partial_{v}\Big)P_{k}^{\ast}(v,\cdot,w)\Big](\rho),\\
&C^{2}_{\sigma}(v,\rho,w):={\rm P.V.}\,\frac{a^{\ast}(v,\rho,w)}{v}A^{\ast}_{k}\Big[P_{k}^{\ast}(v,\cdot,w)\Big](\rho)-A_{k}^{\ast}\Big[{\rm P.V.}\,\frac{a^{\ast}(v,\cdot,w)}{v}P_{k}^{\ast}(v,\cdot,w)\Big](\rho).\end{split}

(9.12)

Simple computation using the regularity of
\zeta
shows

\begin{split}&\big\|\Psi_{9}(\rho)C^{12}_{\sigma}(v,\rho,w)\big\|_{L^{2}(v,\rho,w\in\mathbb{R})}+\big\|\Psi_{9}(\rho)H_{\sigma}(v,\rho,w)\big\|_{L^{2}(v,\rho,w\in\mathbb{R})}\\
&\lesssim\big\|\langle k,\partial_{\rho}\rangle^{-1/2}\partial_{v}A_{k}^{\ast}\big[P_{k}^{\ast}(v,\cdot,w)\big](\rho)\big\|_{L^{2}(v,\rho,w\in\mathbb{R})}.\end{split}

(9.13)

To treat the term
C^{11}_{\sigma}
and
C^{2}_{\sigma}
below, we use the following lemma.

Lemma 9.1
.

Assume that
\sigma\in(0,1)
and
I:=(-20\sigma,-20\sigma)
. Then for any
h_{1},h_{2}\in H^{1}_{0}(I)
we have the bounds

\Big|{\rm P.V.}\,\int_{I}\frac{h_{1}(v)}{v}h_{2}(v)\,dv\Big|\lesssim\sigma^{2/3}\big\|h_{1}\big\|_{H^{1}(I)}\big\|h_{2}\|_{H^{1}(I)}.

(9.14)

Proof of lemma
9.1
.

By Sobolev inequality and interpolation inequality, we have

\big\|h\big\|_{L^{2}(I)}\lesssim\sigma\|\partial_{v}h\|_{L^{2}(I)},\qquad\big\|h\big\|_{H^{2/3}(\mathbb{R})}\lesssim\sigma^{1/3}\|\partial_{v}h\|_{L^{2}(I)},

(9.15)

for any
h\in H^{1}_{0}(I)
. Therefore by Parsevel’s identity we obtain

\begin{split}&\Big|{\rm P.V.}\,\int_{I}\frac{h_{1}(v)}{v}h_{2}(v)\,dv\Big|\lesssim\Big|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\big|\widehat{\,h_{1}}(\xi-\alpha)\big|\big|\widehat{\,h_{2}}(\xi)\big|\,d\alpha d\xi\Big|\\
&\lesssim\big\|h_{1}\big\|_{H^{2/3}(\mathbb{R})}\big\|h_{2}\big\|_{H^{2/3}(\mathbb{R})}\lesssim\sigma^{2/3}\big\|h_{1}\big\|_{H^{1}(I)}\big\|h_{2}\|_{H^{1}(I)}.\end{split}

(9.16)

The lemma is then proved.
∎

Denote for the simplicity of notations

M:=|k|\big\|A^{\ast}_{k}\big[P_{k}^{\ast}(v,\cdot,w)\big](\rho)\big\|_{L^{2}(v,\rho,w\in\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\big\|\partial_{v}A^{\ast}_{k}\big[P_{k}^{\ast}(v,\cdot,w)\big](\rho)\big\|_{L^{2}(v,\rho,w\in\mathbb{R}^{3})}.

(9.17)

Now multiplying
(\Psi_{9}(\rho))^{2}A^{\ast}_{k}\big[P_{k}^{\ast}(v,\cdot,w)\big](\rho)
to (
9.11
), integrating in
v,\rho,w\in\mathbb{R}
, using integration by parts, the bounds (
9.9
), (
9.13
) and lemma
9.1
, and noting that the support of
A^{\ast}_{k}\big[P_{k}^{\ast}(v,\cdot,w)\big](\rho)
is contained in
[-5\sigma,5\sigma]\times\mathbb{R}\times[-5,5]
, we obtain for some
C_{\sigma}\in(0,\infty)
that

\begin{split}&\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Psi_{9}(\rho)\big)^{2}\Big[k^{2}\big|A_{k}^{\ast}\big[P^{\ast}(v,\cdot,w)\big](\rho)\big|^{2}+\big|\partial_{v}A_{k}^{\ast}\big[P^{\ast}(v,\cdot,w)\big](\rho)\big|^{2}\Big]\,dvd\rho dw\\
&\lesssim\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\sigma^{3/2}\big(\Psi_{9}(\rho)\big)^{2}\big|\partial_{v}A_{k}^{\ast}\big[P^{\ast}(v,\cdot,w)\big](\rho)\big|^{2}+\big|\langle k,\partial_{\rho}\rangle^{-1}\partial_{v}A_{k}^{\ast}\big[P^{\ast}(v,\cdot,w)\big](\rho)\big|^{2}\,dvd\rho dw\\
&\qquad+C_{\sigma}\big(\varpi_{\varkappa_{k},w_{\ast}}(w_{\ast},0)\big)^{2}.\end{split}

(9.18)

Noting that
A_{k}^{\ast}\big[P^{\ast}(v,\cdot,w)\big](\rho)=A_{k}^{\ast}\big[\Psi_{9}(\cdot)P^{\ast}(v,\cdot,w)\big](\rho)
, a simple commutator argument shows that for
m\in\{0,1\}
,

\begin{split}&\big\|\Psi_{9}(\rho)\partial^{m}_{v}A_{k}^{\ast}\big[P^{\ast}(v,\cdot,w)\big](\rho)-\partial^{m}_{v}A_{k}^{\ast}\big[P^{\ast}(v,\cdot,w)\big](\rho)\big\|_{L^{2}(v,\rho,w\in\mathbb{R})}\\
&\lesssim\big\|\langle k,\partial_{\rho}\rangle^{-1/2}\partial^{m}_{v}A_{k}^{\ast}\big[P_{k}^{\ast}(v,\cdot,w)\big](\rho)\big\|_{L^{2}(v,\rho,w\in\mathbb{R})}.\end{split}

(9.19)

Combining (
9.18
) and (
9.19
), in view of (
9.8
), we obtain that for any
\gamma\in(0,1)
and suitable
C_{\gamma,\sigma}\in(0,\infty)
,

M\lesssim\sigma^{1/3}M+\gamma M+\big[e^{-\varkappa_{k}|w_{\ast}|}{\bf 1}_{w_{\ast}>0}+e^{-\mu_{\varkappa_{k}}|w_{\ast}|}{\bf 1}_{w_{\ast}\leq 0}\big]C_{\gamma,\sigma}.

(9.20)

Choosing
\sigma,\gamma\in(0,1)
sufficiently small, the desired bounds (
1.53
) then follow from (
9.20
) and (
9.8
). The proof of theorem
1.4
is now complete.

9.2.
Proof of Theorem
1.5

We can now give the proof of Theorem
1.5
using Theorem
1.4
.
Decompose
f_{k}(t,v)
as in (
1.55
), then the bound (
1.57
) on
F_{k,v_{\ast}}^{2}(t,v)
follows directly from the equation (
1.50
) and the bounds (
1.47
) by simple integration by parts in
w
. To prove the bound (
1.57
) on
F_{k,v_{\ast}}^{1}(t,v)
, we observe that for suitable constants
C_{0},C_{1}\in\mathbb{R}
and all
v\in\mathbb{R}
,

F_{k,v_{\ast}}^{1}(t,v)=C_{0}\aleph_{k}(t,v)-C_{1}\Big[D(v)\digamma_{k}(v)-F_{0k}(v)\Big]\Phi_{k}^{\ast}(v-v_{\ast}),

(9.21)

where

\aleph_{k}(t,v):=\Phi_{k}^{\ast}(v-v_{\ast})\int_{\mathbb{R}}e^{-ik(B(w)-B(v))t}{\rm P.V.}\frac{D(v)\Theta_{k}(v-w,w)}{B(v)-B(w)}\Phi^{\ast}(v-w)B^{\prime}(w)\,dw.

(9.22)

It suffices to analyze the smoothness of
\aleph_{k}
in
v
, we make the change of variables for
|\rho-v_{\ast}|\leq 10
,

\nu:=\frac{B(\rho)-B(v_{\ast})}{B^{\prime}(v_{\ast})},\qquad\rho:=\zeta(\nu)+v_{\ast}.

(9.23)

We note that
\zeta
(with suitable extensions if necessary) satisfies the assumptions in (iv) of Theorem
1.4
.
Recalling the identity (
1.48
) and setting for
v,w\in\mathbb{R}
with
|v-v_{\ast}|\leq 10
and
|w-v_{\ast}|\leq 10
,

\nu_{1}(v):=\frac{B(v)-B(v_{\ast})}{B^{\prime}(v_{\ast})},\qquad\nu_{2}(w):=\frac{B(w)-B(v_{\ast})}{B^{\prime}(v_{\ast})},

(9.24)

we note that
\nu_{1}(v)
is Gevrey-2 regular in
v
. We can write

\begin{split}&\aleph_{k}(t,v)=\Phi^{\ast}(v-v_{\ast})D(v)\int_{\mathbb{R}}e^{-ik(B(w)-B(v))t}\,{\rm P.V.}\frac{\Theta_{k}(v-w,w)}{B(v)-B(w)}\Phi^{\ast}(v-w)B^{\prime}(w)\,dw\\
&=\Phi^{\ast}(v-v_{\ast})D(v)\int_{\mathbb{R}}e^{-ik(\nu_{2}-\nu_{1})B^{\prime}(v_{\ast})t}\,{\rm P.V.}\frac{\Theta_{k}\big(\zeta(\nu_{1})-\zeta(\nu_{2}),\zeta(\nu_{2})+v_{\ast}\big)}{\nu_{1}-\nu_{2}}\Phi^{\ast}\big(\zeta(\nu_{1})-\zeta(\nu_{2})\big)\,d\nu_{2}\\
&=\Phi^{\ast}(v-v_{\ast})D(v)\int_{\mathbb{R}}e^{-ik\rho B^{\prime}(v_{\ast})t}\,{\rm P.V.}\frac{\Theta_{k}\big(\zeta(\nu_{1})-\zeta(\nu_{1}+\rho),\zeta(\nu_{1}+\rho)+v_{\ast}\big)}{-\rho}\\
&\hskip 173.44756pt\times\Phi^{\ast}\big(\zeta(\nu_{1})-\zeta(\nu_{1}+\rho)\big)\,d\rho.\end{split}

(9.25)

Set
P(\rho,v):=\Theta_{k}\big(\zeta(\nu_{1})-\zeta(\nu_{1}+\rho),\zeta(\nu_{1}+\rho)+v_{\ast}\big)\Phi^{\ast}\big(\zeta(\nu_{1})-\zeta(\nu_{1}+\rho)\big)\Phi^{\ast\ast}(v-v_{\ast})
. By (
1.52
)-(
1.53
),
P(\rho,v)
is Gevrey-2 regular in
v
(recall the relation (
9.24
)) and satisfies for sufficiently small
\delta_{1}^{\prime\prime}\in(0,1)
depending on
\delta_{1}
,

\big\|(|k|+|\alpha|)e^{2\delta_{1}^{\prime\prime}\langle k,\beta\rangle^{1/2}}\widehat{\,\,P\,\,}(\alpha,\beta)\big\|_{L^{2}(\alpha,\beta\in\mathbb{R})}\lesssim(M_{k}^{\dagger}+|\sigma_{k}|)\varpi_{\varkappa_{k},v_{\ast}}(v_{\ast}).

(9.26)

Therefore, we obtain from (
9.25
)-(
9.26
) that

\begin{split}&\big\|e^{\delta_{1}^{\prime\prime}\langle k,\xi\rangle^{1/2}}\widehat{\,\,\aleph_{k}}(t,\xi)\big\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R})}\lesssim\frac{1}{1+e^{8v_{\ast}}}\Big\|\int_{\mathbb{R}}e^{\delta_{1}^{\prime\prime}\langle k,\xi\rangle^{1/2}}\big|\widehat{\,\,P\,\,}(\alpha,\xi)\big|\,d\alpha\Big\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R})}\lesssim\frac{M_{k}^{\dagger}+|\sigma_{k}|}{1+e^{8v_{\ast}}}\varpi_{\varkappa_{k},v_{\ast}}(v_{\ast}).\end{split}

(9.27)

The desired conclusion then follows from (
9.27
). The theorem is now proved.
